Event ID: 22 (Derived from Section 4.2.1)
Sentence: "g) Monitors a Paging channel for CN paging using 5G-S-TMSI;"

Action: UE decodes PDCCH/PDSCH for paging DCI and checks if the paging record matches its 5G-S-TMSI.
End State: UE either detects a matching paging message (triggering further action) or continues monitoring.

Sentence: "Resume / Release with Suspend"
Start State: UE is in RRC_INACTIVE state (for Resume) or RRC_CONNECTED state (for Release with Suspend).
Condition: For Resume: UE initiates resumption of RRC connection to exchange data or signaling. For Release with Suspend: The network decides to release the RRC connection but retain context to allow faster future resumption.
Action: For Resume: UE sends RRC Resume Request to the network. For Release with Suspend: Network sends RRC Release with Suspend message to the UE, including suspend configuration.
End State: For Resume: UE transitions from RRC_INACTIVE to RRC_CONNECTED state. For Release with Suspend: UE transitions from RRC_CONNECTED to RRC_INACTIVE state with the necessary resume context saved.
============================================================

Action: Not specified (incomplete sentence)
End State: Not specified (incomplete sentence)

Action: Network sends an **RRCRelease** message with **suspendConfig** removed or an explicit release indication.
End State: UE transitions to **RRC_IDLE** state.

Event ID: 132 (Derived from Section 5.2.1)
Sentence: "a) SIBs other than SIB1 and posSIBs are carried in SystemInformation (SI) messages, which are transmitted on the DL-SCH. Only SIBs or posSIBs having the same periodicity can be mapped to the same SI message. SIBs and posSIBs are mapped to the different SI messages.   Each SI message is transmitted within periodically occurring time domain windows (referred to as SI-windows with same length for all SI messages).   Each SI message is associated with an SI-window and the SI-windows of different SI messages do not overlap. That is, within one SI-window only the corresponding SI message is transmitted.   An SI message may be transmitted a number of times within the SI-window.   Any SIB or posSIB except SIB1 can be configured to be cell specific or area specific, using an indication in SIB1.   The cell specific SIB is applicable only within a cell that provides the SIB while the area specific SIB is applicable within an area referred to as SI area, which consists of one or several cells and is identified by systemInformationAreaID;"

End State: UE applies the mapped NR sidelink configurations (SIB12 and sl-ConfigDedicatedNR) as part of RRCReconfiguration.

Event ID: 239 (Derived from Section 5.3.1.2)
Sentence: "RRC integrity protection and ciphering are always activated together, i.e. in one message/procedure. RRC integrity protection and ciphering for SRBs are never de-activated. However, it is possible to switch to a 'NULL' ciphering algorithm (nea0)."
Start State: RRC integrity protection and ciphering are inactive (or in a previous configuration).
Condition: Network decides to activate security for SRBs (e.g., during connection setup or reconfiguration).
Action: Network activates RRC integrity protection and ciphering together in a single procedure (e.g., via `SecurityModeCommand`). If needed, ciphering can be set to 'NULL' (nea0) while keeping integrity protection active.
End State: RRC integrity protection is active, and ciphering is either active (with a non-NULL algorithm) or set to 'NULL' (nea0). SRB security remains permanently enabled (no deactivation).

Event ID: 246 (Derived from Section 5.3.1.2)
Sentence: "It is not allowed to use the same COUNT value more than once for a given security key. As specified in TS 33.501 clause 6.9.4.1 , the network is responsible for avoiding reuse of the COUNT with the same RB identity and with the same key, e.g. due to the transfer of large volumes of data, release and establishment of new RBs, and multiple termination point changes for RLC-UM bearers and multiple termination point changes for RLC-AM bearer with SN terminated PDCP re-establishment (COUNT reset) due to SN only full configuration whilst the key stream inputs (i.e.bearer ID, security key) at MN have not been updated. In order to avoid such re-use, the network may e.g. use different RB identities for RB establishments, change the AS security key, or an RRC_CONNECTED to RRC_IDLE/RRC_INACTIVE and then to RRC_CONNECTED transition."

Sentence: "The purpose of this procedure is to resume a suspended RRC connection, including resuming SRB(s), DRB(s) and multicast MRB(s) or perform an RNA update. This procedure is also used to initiate SDT in RRC_INACTIVE."
Start State: UE is in **RRC_INACTIVE** state with a suspended RRC connection.
Condition: UE needs to resume communication, either due to: Uplink data availability, Incoming paging, RNA (RAN-based Notification Area) update, or Initiation of Small Data Transmission (SDT).
Action: UE initiates the RRC resume procedure, which: Resumes SRB(s), DRB(s), and multicast MRB(s) if previously configured, or Performs an RNA update, or Initiates SDT in RRC_INACTIVE state.
End State: UE transitions to RRC_CONNECTED state or remains in RRC_INACTIVE if only RNA update or SDT is performed, with resources and security contexts restored as applicable.

Event ID: 655 (Derived from Section 1)
Sentence: "Mn + Ocn + Hys &lt; Ms + Ocs + Off"
Start State: UE is in connected mode (RRC_CONNECTED) and performing measurements.
Condition: The serving cell measurement (Mn) with offset (Ocn) and hysteresis (Hys) is less than the neighboring cell measurement (Ms) with offset (Ocs) and a bias (Off).
Action: UE evaluates the condition for potential cell reselection or handover.
End State: If the condition is met, UE may trigger a measurement report or initiate a handover procedure.

Similarly, the eNB needs the capabilities for RAT types eutra and eutra-nr and it uses the featureSetsEUTRA in the UE-EUTRA- Capability together with the featureSetCombinations in the UE-MRDC-Capability to determine the E- UTRA UE capabilities for the supported MRDC band combinations. Hence, the IDs used in the featureSets must match the IDs referred to in featureSetCombinations across all three containers. The requirement on consistency implies that there are no undefined feature sets and feature set combinations."

Sentence: "For unicast of NR sidelink communication, AS security comprises of integrity protection of PC5 signalling (SL-SRB1, SL-SRB2 and SL-SRB3) and user data (SL-DRBs), and it further comprises of ciphering of PC5 signaling (SL-SRB1 only for the Direct Link Security Mode Complete message as specified in TS 24.587, SL-SRB2 and SL-SRB3) and user data (SL-DRBs). Event ID: 1170 (Derived from Section 5.9.1.1)
Sentence: "MBS broadcast configuration information is provided on MCCH logical channel. MCCH carries the MBSBroadcastConfiguration message which indicates the MBS broadcast sessions that are provided in the cell as well as the corresponding scheduling related information for these sessions. Optionally, the MBSBroadcastConfiguration message may also contain a list of neighbour cells providing the same broadcast MBS service(s) as provided in the current cell. The configuration information required by the UE to receive MCCH is provided in SIB20. Additionally, System Information provides also an information related to service continuity of MBS broadcast in SIB21."
Start State: Not specified

Action: Not specified
End State: Not specified

Event ID: 1171 (Derived from Section 5.9.1.2)
Sentence: "The MCCH information (i.e. information transmitted in messages sent over MCCH) is transmitted periodically, using a configurable repetition period and within a configured transmission window. MCCH transmissions (and the associated radio resources and MCS) are indicated via the PDCCH addressed to MCCH-RNTI. PDCCH monitoring occasion(s) for MCCH transmission are determined according to the common search space indicated by searchspaceMCCH. If searchspaceMCCH is set to zero, PDCCH monitoring occasions for MCCH message reception in the MCCH transmission window are the same as PDCCH monitoring occasions for SIB1 where the mapping between PDCCH monitoring occasions and SSBs is specified in TS 38.213. If searchspaceMCCH is not set to zero, PDCCH monitoring occasions for MCCH message are determined based on search space indicated by searchspaceMCCH."
Start State: Not specified
Condition: Not specified
Action: Not specified
End State: Not specified
============================================================
Event ID: 1172 (Derived from Section 5.9.1.2)
Sentence: "PDCCH monitoring occasions for MCCH message which are not overlapping with UL symbols (determined according to tdd-UL-DL-ConfigurationCommon) are sequentially numbered from one in the MCCH transmission window. The [xN+K]th PDCCH monitoring occasion for MCCH message in MCCH transmission window corresponds to the Kth transmitted SSB, where x = 0, 1, ...X-1, K = 1, 2, N, N is the number of actual transmitted SSBs determined according to ssb-PositionsInBurst in SIB1 and X is equal to CEIL(number of PDCCH monitoring occasions in MCCH transmission window/N). The actual transmitted SSBs are sequentially numbered from one in ascending order of their SSB indexes. The UE assumes that, in the MCCH transmisson window, PDCCH for an MCCH message is transmitted in at least one PDCCH monitoring occasion corresponding to each transmitted SSB and thus the selection of SSB for the reception MCCH messages is up to UE implementation."
Start State: Not specified
Condition: Not specified
Action: Not specified
End State: Not specified
============================================================

Event ID: 1176 (Derived from Section 5.9.1.3)
Sentence: "Upon receiving a change notification, a UE receiving or interested to receive MBS services transmitted using MBS broadcast acquires the new MCCH information starting from the same slot. The UE applies the previously acquired MCCH information until the UE acquires the new MCCH information. The notification is transmitted with a 2-bit bitmap, see TS 38.212 clause 7.3.1.2.1. The MSB in the 2-bit bitmap, when set to '1', indicates the start of MBS service(s). The LSB in the 2-bit bitmap, when set to '1', indicates modification of MCCH information other than the change caused by start of new MBS service(s), e.g. modification of a configuration of an on-going MBS session(s), MBS session(s) stop or neighbouring cell information modification."
Start State: Not specified
Condition: Not specified
Action: Not specified
End State: Not specified

Event ID: 1179 (Derived from Section 5.9.2.2)
Sentence: "Unless explicitly stated otherwise in the procedural specification, the MCCH information acquisition procedure overwrites any stored MCCH information, i.e. delta configuration is not applicable for MCCH information and the UE discontinues using a field if it is absent in MCCH information."
Start State: UE has stored MCCH information (if any).
Condition: MCCH information acquisition procedure is triggered (e.g., upon MCCH change notification or UE (re-)selection to a cell).
Action: UE overwrites the stored MCCH information with the newly acquired one. If any field is absent in the new MCCH, the UE stops using that field. Delta update (incremental config) is not applied—full replacement is performed.
End State: UE has updated MCCH information, and unused fields (if absent) are no longer applied.

Event ID: 1195 (Derived from Section 5.9.4.5)
Sentence: "The UE shall set the contents of the MBS Interest Indication as follows:"
Start State: UE is in RRC_CONNECTED state and supports MBS (Multicast/Broadcast Services).
Condition: Network requests or UE autonomously decides to update MBS interest (e.g., due to changed service preferences or network configuration).
Action: UE populates the MBS Interest Indication message fields (e.g., `mbs-InterestIndication-r17`) per 3GPP TS 38.331 specifications.
End State: UE sends the MBS Interest Indication to the network, enabling network-aware MBS resource allocation.
